MCMC - Chapter 219

1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any mcmc module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 663. The projective resolution has length at most 928 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 663 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the mcmc structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 928. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
